| **Otta** | A curated job platform for tech professionals, focusing on company mission, culture, and impact. | Tech workers who prioritize purpose and culture over pure salary. | Cuts through the noise of generic tech boards by highlighting startups with strong missions and transparent cultures. | [otta.com](https://otta.com) |
| **Working Nomads** | Curates remote job listings from across the web and sends them in a weekly newsletter. | Remote workers who want a hand-picked, low-volume feed of quality opportunities. | Low-tech, high-signal resource. Avoids the overwhelm of large job boards; the curation is the key. | [workingnomads.co](https://www.workingnomads.co) |

| **Pilgrim** (UK) | Connects students and graduates with job opportunities and internships in the UK and abroad. | Students and graduates in the UK seeking international opportunities. | Focuses on entry-level roles with international companies; often includes visa sponsorship. | [pilgrim.co.uk](https://www.pilgrim.co.uk) |
| **FreshGigs.ca** (Canada) | Focuses on entry-level and junior roles in tech and creative fields. | New graduates in Canada targeting tech and creative industries. | Specializes in junior positions that often come with training and potential sponsorship. | [freshgigs.ca](https://freshgigs.ca) |
| **StarterJobs** (France) | French job board for first jobs and early careers. | French-speaking graduates entering the job market. | Focuses on entry-level positions in France, some with international exposure. | [starterjobs.com](https://www.starterjobs.com) |
| **EuroBrussels** | The primary job board for EU institutions, international organizations, and NGOs based in Brussels. | Professionals seeking careers in EU policy, law, administration, and international relations. | The single best place for EU institution jobs (Commission, Parliament, Agencies), but hyper-specific to Brussels. | [eurobrussels.com](https://www.eurobrussels.com) |
| **Bayt.com** | The largest and most popular job site in the Middle East & North Africa (MENA) region. | Professionals targeting careers in the Gulf (UAE, Saudi, Qatar) and the broader MENA region. | The regional giant for the Middle East, completely invisible to Western job board aggregators. | [bayt.com](https://www.bayt.com) |
| **GulfTalent** | Leading recruitment site for the Gulf Cooperation Council (GCC) countries. | Professionals seeking jobs in Saudi Arabia, UAE, Qatar, Kuwait, Bahrain, and Oman. | Specializes in GCC job market; many roles come with tax-free income and relocation packages. | [gulftalent.com](https://www.gulftalent.com) |

| **Careers.IN** | Major Indian job portal with extensive listings across all sectors. | Professionals targeting the Indian job market, including international companies in India. | One of India's largest job portals; good for remote roles with US/EU companies hiring from India. | [careers.in](https://www.careers.in) |
| **Naukrigulf** | From the Naukri.com network, focused on Gulf jobs. | Indian and international professionals seeking jobs in the Gulf region. | Leverages Naukri's extensive database for Gulf opportunities; popular among Indian expats. | [naukrigulf.com](https://www.naukrigulf.com) |
| **Jobberman** | Leading job site in West Africa (Nigeria, Ghana, Kenya). | Professionals targeting West African markets. | The go-to platform for Nigeria and Ghana; strong focus on local and regional opportunities. | [jobberman.com](https://www.jobberman.com) |

| **PeoplePerHour** | UK-based freelancing platform for project-based work, with a growing number of remote listings. | Freelancers and consultants in digital, creative, and business fields. | Popular in Europe; many UK and EU clients post remote-friendly project work. | [peopleperhour.com](https://www.peopleperhour.com) |
| **Freelancer.com** | Massive global freelancing platform with a huge variety of job postings, from simple tasks to complex projects. | Freelancers and businesses of all sizes seeking project-based work. | The sheer volume means many international clients post remote-friendly projects. | [freelancer.com](https://www.freelancer.com) |
| **Guru** | Freelance platform with focus on long-term relationships and a "SafePay" escrow system. | Freelancers seeking reliable clients and secure payment. | Emphasizes building ongoing relationships, which can lead to stable remote contract work. | [guru.com](https://www.guru.com) |

## **SCRAPING STRATEGY FOR YOUR BOT** 🤖

**Priority 1 (High Value, Technical Feasibility):**
1. Indeed (RSS feeds available)
2. LinkedIn (tough but necessary—use API or stealth)
3. We Work Remotely (easy scrape, clean structure)
4. RemoteOK (simple API/structure)
5. AngelList (structured, startup focus)
6. GitHub Jobs (if still active)
7. Stack Overflow Jobs (developer gold)
8. Hacker News "Who is Hiring" (monthly scrape)
9. FlexJobs (paid but worth it)
10. Government boards (USAJobs, Job Bank Canada—often have APIs)

**Priority 2 (Niche Verticals):**
- Dice (tech)
- Behance/Dribbble (creative)
- eFinancialCareers (finance)
- HigherEdJobs (academia)
- Idealist (non-profit)
- Mediabistro (media)
- AllHealthcareJobs (health)

**Priority 3 (International Expansion):**
- Naukri (India)
- Seek (AU/NZ)
- StepStone (EU)
- JobStreet (SE Asia)
- InfoJobs (LATAM/Spain)

**APIs Available (Easy Mode):**
- Indeed Job Search API (limited free tier)
- LinkedIn Marketing API (restricted)
- GitHub Jobs API (deprecated but check)
- USAJobs API (federal government—open)
- Adzuna API (aggregator—available)
- CareerJet API (available)
- Jooble API (available)
